**Cases:** POL-C04. **Evidence:** scholarly-analysis. **Confidence:** high. **Causal status:** descriptive.

- [POL-S11: Les origines du système métrique en France et la Convention du mètre de 1875, qui a ouvert la voie au Système international d’unités et à sa révision de 2018](https://comptes-rendus.academie-sciences.fr/physique/item/10.1016/j.crhy.2018.12.002.pdf), pp. 13–14, quoted articles 1 and 3. Supports: Repeal and effective date.
- [POL-S13: A History of the Metric System Controversy in the United States](https://nvlpubs.nist.gov/nistpubs/Legacy/SP/nbsspecialpublication345-10.pdf), pp. 10–11, note 53. Supports: Translated prohibitions including commercial/legal settings.

**Diagnostic test:**

- **status:** descriptive-only
- **reason:** Corroborated legal sequence; explicit non-equivalence to use.

**Counterevidence/rival:** Older words and task measures persisted according to the Isère synthesis.

**Inference limit:** Both accounts derive from the same law; original Bulletin des lois not inspected. No national compliance denominator.

## POL-K18

The Isère archival synthesis describes customary measures tied to agricultural work and continued rural usage beyond the 1840 legal deadline; practical task fit is a plausible part of persistence, not a measured universal preference.

**Cases:** POL-C04. **Evidence:** institutional-synthesis. **Confidence:** medium. **Causal status:** mechanism-supported.

- [POL-S12: Faites poids et mesures…](https://archives.isere.fr/sites/isere-archives-fr/files/inline-files/ca_13_poids_mesures_protege.pdf), PDF pp. 2, 6–9, especially journal/land-work discussion and long-run coda. Supports: Task-linked area measures, local implementation and later coexistence.

**Diagnostic test:**

- **status:** underdetermined
- **mechanism chain:** Established work/commodity conventions -> customary units remain useful for local tasks -> continued mixed practice despite a legal standard.
- **strongest rival:** Pure inertia or educational exclusion, with no continuing practical utility.
- **discriminating observation:** Contexts where a task-linked measure persists as the task persists and changes when work changes, despite ability to use metric units.
- **evidence sought found:** S12 links the journal to horse-ploughing and describes later rural coexistence; no individual longitudinal literacy/use evidence.
- **remaining ambiguity:** Source offers qualitative association, not a discriminating controlled comparison.
- **hypothesis timing:** emerged during research

**Counterevidence/rival:** Habit, limited schooling, fraud and political resistance may also explain use; the source does not isolate these channels.

**Inference limit:** Coda extends beyond 1795–1840 and is used only to reject instantaneous displacement. Neither continued terminology nor local examples establish unchanged quantities nationally.

## SYN-K01

A use-centred historical research design includes persistence, coexistence, repair and alternatives that invention-centred timelines can omit.

**Cases:** method/synthesis. **Evidence:** scholarly-analysis. **Confidence:** high. **Causal status:** descriptive.

- [SYN-S01: The Shock of the Old: Technology and Global History since 1900 — Introduction (inspected excerpt)](https://sidoli.w.waseda.jp/Edgerton_2008_01.pdf), Introduction pp. xi–xv. Supports: Author explicitly argues for technology-in-use as an alternative historiographical focus.

**Diagnostic test:**

- **status:** descriptive-only
- **reason:** Claim describes an author’s research design or explicit argument, not an identified adoption mechanism.

**Counterevidence/rival:** Invention-focused work remains useful for questions about novelty; this is a design argument, not a quantified bias estimate.

**Inference limit:** Does not validate each historical example in the book.

## SYN-K02

Comin and Mestieri distinguish estimated adoption timing from intensity of technology use; their growth accounting is model-based and uses a selected 25-technology, 139-country sample.

**Cases:** method/synthesis. **Evidence:** scholarly-analysis. **Confidence:** high. **Causal status:** descriptive.

- [SYN-S02: If Technology Has Arrived Everywhere, Why Has Income Diverged?](https://dcomin.host.dartmouth.edu/Publications_files/CM_rev_71917.pdf), Introduction pp.1–3; §4.1 p.18. Supports: Defines two diffusion margins, sample and modelling approach.

**Diagnostic test:**

- **status:** descriptive-only
- **reason:** Claim describes an author’s research design or explicit argument, not an identified adoption mechanism.

**Counterevidence/rival:** Their representativeness assumption is not independently established by this programme.

**Inference limit:** Does not establish why diffusion differs or warrant exporting estimated lags to all technologies.

## SYN-K03

Rosenberg explicitly treats diffusion as necessary to understanding the economic consequences of technological change rather than equating invention with those consequences.

**Cases:** method/synthesis. **Evidence:** scholarly-analysis. **Confidence:** medium. **Causal status:** descriptive.

- [SYN-S03: Factors affecting the diffusion of technology](https://www.cambridge.org/core/books/abs/perspectives-on-technology/factors-affecting-the-diffusion-of-technology/C129CAACB1FEFC00B840505B2C20D8D5), Publisher summary. Supports: Summary states why adoption and displacement rates matter.

**Diagnostic test:**

- **status:** descriptive-only
- **reason:** Claim describes an author’s research design or explicit argument, not an identified adoption mechanism.

**Counterevidence/rival:** Detailed chapter was not inspected.

**Inference limit:** A methodological proposition, not evidence for a particular adoption mechanism.

## SYN-K10

In the selected cases, a described working principle, household device availability and equipment delivery do not establish operational scale, control of access or sustained beneficial use.

**Cases:** AGR-C07; ANC-C01; DIG-C04. **Evidence:** synthesis-inference. **Confidence:** medium. **Causal status:** mechanism-supported.

- [ANC-S01: The Pneumatics of Hero of Alexandria from the original Greek](https://www.gutenberg.org/files/77400/77400-h/77400-h.htm), §50, p. 72; preface pp. vii–viii. Supports: Bounded evidence in ANC-K01: Mechanism and editorial framing of the inspected edition.
- [DIG-S07: Annual Status of Education Report (Rural) 2021](https://img.asercentre.org/docs/aser2021forweb.pdf), Table 10 p.28; Table 12 p.31. Supports: Bounded evidence in DIG-K13: Conditional access shares.
- [AGR-S12: Up in Smoke: The Influence of Household Behavior on the Long-Run Impact of Improved Cooking Stoves (2012 working paper)](https://www.hks.harvard.edu/sites/default/files/centers/cid/files/publications/faculty-working-papers/241_HannaDufloGreenstone.pdf), sections V–VII; discussion and conclusion, printed pp. 27–32. Supports: Bounded evidence in AGR-K18: Usage, maintenance, fuel, time, health and interpretation of the real programme's performance.

**Diagnostic test:**

- **status:** evaluated
- **mechanism chain:** Observed artifact/access -> distinct deployment and use constraints -> different downstream outcomes; no automatic stage transition.
- **strongest rival:** A simple technical-availability account predicts that providing an effective object is enough for the claimed downstream outcome.
- **discriminating observation:** Find documented configurations with the object/principle present but the relevant downstream use unestablished or constrained.
- **evidence sought found:** Parent compared the inspected case claims ANC-K01, DIG-K13, AGR-K18. Source acquisition is documented by their authors; independent sample audits are recorded separately in reviews/.
- **remaining ambiguity:** The cases observe different stages, not matched treatment effects; Hero’s absence of load is a source limit, not proof of no ancient application.
- **hypothesis timing:** retrospective synthesis

**Counterevidence/rival:** A simple technical-availability account predicts that providing an effective object is enough for the claimed downstream outcome.

**Inference limit:** The cases observe different stages, not matched treatment effects; Hero’s absence of load is a source limit, not proof of no ancient application.

## SYN-K11

Material performance and production methods are task-dependent in these comparisons; older practices and newer inputs can coexist or combine rather than form a universal replacement sequence.

**Cases:** IND-C07; MAT-C05; NET-C02. **Evidence:** synthesis-inference. **Confidence:** medium. **Causal status:** mechanism-supported.

- [MAT-S08: Arsenic Bronze: Dirty Copper or Chosen Alloy? A View from the Americas](https://www.academia.edu/31356952/Lechtman_Arsenic_Bronze_Dirty_Copper_1996), pp. 500–508; Tables 4–6; Figs. 25–27. Supports: Bounded evidence in MAT-K15: Mechanical tests and comparison with thin-sheet fabrication traditions.
- [NET-S05: Introduction, The Book Worlds of East Asia and Europe, 1450–1850: Connections and Comparisons](https://hkupress.hku.hk/image/catalog/pdf-preview/9789888208081.pdf), pp. 11–14, Technology Transfer. Supports: Bounded evidence in NET-K07: Division of labour, later reprinting from blocks, paper/premises and financing comparisons.
- [IND-S15: Women’s Work and the Politics of Homespun in Socialist China, 1949–1980](https://www.cambridge.org/core/services/aop-cambridge-core/content/view/1B4DC63727682F1EEC958A62A4126757/S0020859012000521a.pdf/div-class-title-womenandapos-s-work-and-the-politics-of-homespun-in-socialist-china-1949-1980-div.pdf), pp. 373–374, notes 23–27. Supports: Bounded evidence in IND-K20: Describes mixed yarn use, Dingxian and geographical expansion of hand weaving.

**Diagnostic test:**

- **status:** evaluated
- **mechanism chain:** Different required outputs and workflows -> different useful properties -> selective adoption or hybrid production.
- **strongest rival:** A single technical-superiority ranking predicts broadly uniform replacement once the newer option is available.
- **discriminating observation:** Compare measured material trade-offs and documented mixed workflows or specialised uses.
- **evidence sought found:** Parent compared the inspected case claims MAT-K15, NET-K07, IND-K20. Source acquisition is documented by their authors; independent sample audits are recorded separately in reviews/.
- **remaining ambiguity:** Coexistence alone does not prove optimal choice or consent; prices, exclusion and unavailable alternatives may also explain persistence.
- **hypothesis timing:** retrospective synthesis

**Counterevidence/rival:** A single technical-superiority ranking predicts broadly uniform replacement once the newer option is available.

**Inference limit:** Coexistence alone does not prove optimal choice or consent; prices, exclusion and unavailable alternatives may also explain persistence.

## SYN-K12

Chooser, payer, operator and affected person cannot be assumed identical: the selected cases show altered control over output, coerced maintenance work and provider revenue incentives.

**Cases:** AGR-C03; ANC-C03; MED-C08. **Evidence:** synthesis-inference. **Confidence:** medium. **Causal status:** mechanism-supported.

- [AGR-S05: Contract Farming and Female Rice Growers in The Gambia](https://media.odi.org/documents/8169.pdf), sections 3–5, pp. 4–10. Supports: Bounded evidence in AGR-K07: Pre-project rights, project allocation, reclassification and household labour obligations.
- [ANC-S05: The Aqueducts of Rome (De aquaeductu urbis Romae), Book II](https://penelope.uchicago.edu/Thayer/E/Roman/Texts/Frontinus/De_Aquis/Bennett/2%2A.html), §§116–117. Supports: Bounded evidence in ANC-K08: Reported headcounts, ownership and functions.
- [MED-S15: Addressing Antibiotic Abuse in China: An Experimental Audit Study](https://pmc.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/articles/PMC4776334/), §I results; §III experiment 1 protocol. Supports: Bounded evidence in MED-K22: Randomly assigned standardized-patient roles and reported encounter rates.

**Diagnostic test:**

- **status:** evaluated
- **mechanism chain:** Decision rights/revenue/labour obligations -> actor-specific choices -> adoption and benefits/harms distributed across different people.
- **strongest rival:** A representative voluntary adopter with a common welfare objective is sufficient to explain observed uptake.
- **discriminating observation:** Look for directly documented labour status, entitlement changes or an intervention altering one actor’s incentive.
- **evidence sought found:** Parent compared the inspected case claims AGR-K07, ANC-K08, MED-K22. Source acquisition is documented by their authors; independent sample audits are recorded separately in reviews/.
- **remaining ambiguity:** These cases establish heterogeneous interests, not a general effect size of coercion or a complete welfare balance.
- **hypothesis timing:** retrospective synthesis

**Counterevidence/rival:** A representative voluntary adopter with a common welfare objective is sufficient to explain observed uptake.

**Inference limit:** These cases establish heterogeneous interests, not a general effect size of coercion or a complete welfare balance.

## SYN-K13

Recurring operating and maintenance arrangements matter in these cases, but the evidence does not isolate maintenance as the universal or unique cause of persistence or reversal.

**Cases:** AGR-C07; ANC-C02; MED-C06. **Evidence:** synthesis-inference. **Confidence:** medium. **Causal status:** mechanism-supported.

- [AGR-S12: Up in Smoke: The Influence of Household Behavior on the Long-Run Impact of Improved Cooking Stoves (2012 working paper)](https://www.hks.harvard.edu/sites/default/files/centers/cid/files/publications/faculty-working-papers/241_HannaDufloGreenstone.pdf), sections V–VII; discussion and conclusion, printed pp. 27–32. Supports: Bounded evidence in AGR-K18: Usage, maintenance, fuel, time, health and interpretation of the real programme's performance.
- [ANC-S04: Operation and decline of the Barbegal mill complex, the largest industrial complex of antiquity](https://doi.org/10.1002/gea.22016), §5 discussion of flumes A–C, Fig. 9; §6 Conclusions, pp. 604–607. Supports: Bounded evidence in ANC-K06: Different terminal sequences and continued water flow.
- [MED-S11: Analysis of Historical Trends and Recent Elimination of Malaria from Sri Lanka and Its Applicability for Malaria Control in Other Countries](https://www.frontiersin.org/journals/public-health/articles/10.3389/fpubh.2017.00212/full), Malaria in Sri Lanka During the Second Half of the Twentieth Century. Supports: Bounded evidence in MED-K15: Counts and programme sequence.
- [MED-S12: Malaria resurgence: a systematic review and assessment of its causes](https://link.springer.com/article/10.1186/1475-2875-11-122), Weakening of the malaria control programme, Sri Lanka paragraph. Supports: Bounded evidence in MED-K15: Same sequence with surveillance and population-movement qualifications.

**Diagnostic test:**

- **status:** underdetermined
- **mechanism chain:** Recurring skill/supply/work changes -> usable service changes -> altered retention or effective operation.
- **strongest rival:** A durable installation or successful initial intervention alone secures continuing service; alternatively demand/ecology rather than upkeep explains decline.
- **discriminating observation:** Trace equipment condition, recurring provision and service outcomes; distinguish continued inputs from actual operation.
- **evidence sought found:** Parent compared the inspected case claims AGR-K18, ANC-K06, MED-K15. Source acquisition is documented by their authors; independent sample audits are recorded separately in reviews/.
- **remaining ambiguity:** Multiple variables change together; retrospective decline explanations may share records and remain underdetermined.
- **hypothesis timing:** retrospective synthesis

**Counterevidence/rival:** A durable installation or successful initial intervention alone secures continuing service; alternatively demand/ecology rather than upkeep explains decline.

**Inference limit:** Multiple variables change together; retrospective decline explanations may share records and remain underdetermined.

## SYN-K14

Risk, labour over a full production cycle and complete-journey costs can change the relevant adoption comparison; acquisition price or one performance denominator is insufficient in these cases.

**Cases:** AGR-C01; AGR-C06; MOB-C01. **Evidence:** synthesis-inference. **Confidence:** medium. **Causal status:** mechanism-supported.

- [AGR-S11: Agricultural Decisions after Relaxing Credit and Risk Constraints](https://www.atai-research.org/wp-content/uploads/2016/07/Agricultural-Decisions-after-Relaxing-Credit-and-Risk-Constraints.pdf), pp. 599–601; experiment description and investment results, including tables III–IV. Supports: Bounded evidence in AGR-K15: Randomised cash/insurance interventions and measured farm investment responses.
- [AGR-S02: Energetic efficiency and political expediency in Titicaca Basin raised field agriculture](https://www.academia.edu/6455173/Energetic_Efficiency_and_Political_Expediency_in_Titicaca_Basin_Raised_Field_Agriculture), pp. 280–288. Supports: Bounded evidence in AGR-K03: Energy/labour accounting, rotation comparisons, heterogeneous reported yields and political-economic interpretations.
- [MOB-S02: The costs of transporting goods by different modes: A case study of pottery movement in late Roman Britain](https://api.repository.cam.ac.uk/server/api/core/bitstreams/7291f9ad-f581-44cb-9e21-a63889ffd40b/content), §1.1 pp. 1–2 and §4.4 pp. 8–9. Supports: Bounded evidence in MOB-K03: Explicit chain of transport costs and critique of comparisons omitting transfers.
- [MOB-S01: ORBIS: The Stanford Geospatial Network Model of the Roman World, version dated 2 May 2012](https://orbis.stanford.edu/orbis2012/ORBIS_v1paper_20120501.pdf), p. 7 and p. 17. Supports: Bounded evidence in MOB-K03: ORBIS warns expense data are schematic and uses a price-edict sailing-time calibration.

**Diagnostic test:**

- **status:** underdetermined
- **mechanism chain:** Uncounted risk, recurring labour or transfer costs -> different feasible payoff for the task -> changed investment or comparative ranking.
- **strongest rival:** Nominal purchase price, initial yield per hectare or one transport-leg cost alone ranks the alternatives.
- **discriminating observation:** Vary risk separately from cash, include full rotations, and compare complete journeys rather than isolated legs.
- **evidence sought found:** Parent compared the inspected case claims AGR-K15, AGR-K03, MOB-K03. Source acquisition is documented by their authors; independent sample audits are recorded separately in reviews/.
- **remaining ambiguity:** The insurance experiment, contested labour model and archaeological cost inference have different identification strengths and cannot be pooled.
- **hypothesis timing:** retrospective synthesis

**Counterevidence/rival:** Nominal purchase price, initial yield per hectare or one transport-leg cost alone ranks the alternatives.

**Inference limit:** The insurance experiment, contested labour model and archaeological cost inference have different identification strengths and cannot be pooled.

## SYN-K16

Aggregate opportunities or productivity gains do not establish accessible gains for displaced individuals: incumbent outcomes, task relocation and bargaining arrangements require separate evidence.

**Cases:** LBR-C01; MOB-C06. **Evidence:** synthesis-inference. **Confidence:** medium. **Causal status:** mechanism-supported.

- [LBR-S02: Answering the Call of Automation: How the Labor Market Adjusted to Mechanizing Telephone Operation](https://www.dpgross.com/papers/answering-call-automation.pdf), §VI, §VII and conclusion p.1934. Supports: Bounded evidence in LBR-K02: Linked incumbent outcomes differ from repeated-cohort employment findings.
- [MOB-S12: Volkswagenwerk Aktiengesellschaft v. Federal Maritime Commission, 390 U.S. 261](https://www.law.cornell.edu/supremecourt/text/390/261), opening paragraphs 2–4, 390 U.S. 261. Supports: Bounded evidence in MOB-K22: Agreement, fund, and weight-versus-volume assessment dispute.
- [MOB-S13: California Cartage Co. v. NLRB, 822 F.2d 1203](https://law.justia.com/cases/federal/appellate-courts/F2/822/1203/373792/), part I, paragraphs describing break-bulk, 1960 agreement and off-dock container work. Supports: Bounded evidence in MOB-K23: Court recounts Board findings of greater-than-expected work loss and the dispute over a fifty-mile work-preservation agreement.

**Diagnostic test:**

- **status:** evaluated
- **mechanism chain:** Technology shifts tasks -> workers differ in exposure/entry rights -> compensation, reallocation and outcomes diverge.
- **strongest rival:** New work or aggregate employment resilience automatically compensates those whose tasks disappear.
- **discriminating observation:** Track incumbents separately from entrants and identify how task jurisdiction and compensation actually changed.
- **evidence sought found:** Parent compared the inspected case claims LBR-K02, MOB-K22, MOB-K23. Source acquisition is documented by their authors; independent sample audits are recorded separately in reviews/.
- **remaining ambiguity:** US labour cases are not global replications; bargaining records do not identify every worker’s realised transition or welfare.
- **hypothesis timing:** retrospective synthesis

**Counterevidence/rival:** New work or aggregate employment resilience automatically compensates those whose tasks disappear.

**Inference limit:** US labour cases are not global replications; bargaining records do not identify every worker’s realised transition or welfare.

## SYN-K17

The source audits identify consequential differences between measures and causal claims: VR gaming reports, ORT episode definitions and container adoption-timing estimates cannot be treated as interchangeable general adoption evidence.

**Cases:** DIG-C06; MED-C07; MOB-C06. **Evidence:** synthesis-inference. **Confidence:** medium. **Causal status:** contested.

- [DIG-S15: Adults’ Media Literacy Core Survey 2023 Data Tables](https://www.ofcom.org.uk/siteassets/resources/documents/research-and-data/data/statistics/2024/adults-media-literacy-tracker/adults-media-literacy-core-survey-2023-data-tables?v=330749), Table 74 G 1 pp.307–308; fieldwork header. Supports: Bounded evidence in DIG-K18: Matched device question, levels and 3, 643 base.
- [DIG-S16: Adults’ Media Literacy Research 2023 Technical Report](https://www.ofcom.org.uk/siteassets/resources/documents/research-and-data/data/statistics/2024/adults-media-literacy-tracker/adults-media-literacy-tracker-2023-technical-report?v=330751), pp.7–8. Supports: Bounded evidence in DIG-K18: 2, 121 face-to-face plus 1, 522 online quota interviews; adult weighting.
- [MED-S14: The status of ORT in Bangladesh: how widely is it used?](https://doi.org/10.1093/heapol/12.1.58), p. 58 abstract; p. 62 Table 2 footnote; pp. 64–65 Discussion. Supports: Bounded evidence in MED-K21: Direct denominator comparison; broad ORT definition includes home fluids.
- [MOB-S11: Estimating the effects of the container revolution on world trade](https://qn1-next.xuetangonline.com/16800654811573.pdf), p. 37; Table 2 pp. 43–44; §3.4 pp. 45–46. Supports: Bounded evidence in MOB-K21: Pre-trend failures and endogeneity warning; benchmark 17.4% North–North product differential and approximately 14% world differential with delayed accrual.

**Diagnostic test:**

- **status:** evaluated
- **mechanism chain:** Observation definition/identification strategy -> reported quantity -> permissible interpretation.
- **strongest rival:** Headline summaries preserve sufficient information for a cross-technology success ranking.
- **discriminating observation:** Inspect original question wording, table denominators and published identification cautions.
- **evidence sought found:** Parent compared the inspected case claims DIG-K18, MED-K21, MOB-K21. Source acquisition is documented by their authors; independent sample audits are recorded separately in reviews/.
- **remaining ambiguity:** These purposively selected discrepancies do not estimate the error rate of the entire bibliography.
- **hypothesis timing:** retrospective synthesis

**Counterevidence/rival:** Headline summaries preserve sufficient information for a cross-technology success ranking.

**Inference limit:** These purposively selected discrepancies do not estimate the error rate of the entire bibliography.

## SYN-K18

The acquired digital evidence leaves the universal portability-versus-performance ranking and the “most laptops cheaper than most smartphones” assertion unestablished; it supports only bounded task and use comparisons.

**Cases:** DIG-C03; DIG-C04; DIG-C06. **Evidence:** synthesis-inference. **Confidence:** medium. **Causal status:** contested.

- [DIG-S04: Communications Market Report 2015: Internet and online content](https://www.ofcom.org.uk/siteassets/resources/documents/research-and-data/cmr/cmr15/uk_5.pdf?v=322209), Figure 5.20. Supports: Bounded evidence in DIG-K24: Reported importance, without component intervention or price distributions.
- [DIG-S05: The Smartphone Difference / U.S. Smartphone Use in 2015](https://www.pewresearch.org/internet/wp-content/uploads/sites/9/2015/03/PI_Smartphones_0401151.pdf), Smartphone dependence and financial constraints. Supports: Bounded evidence in DIG-K24: Constrained access is a rival to pure preference.
- [DIG-S06: Telecom Statistics India-2024](https://www.dot.gov.in/static/uploads/2025/07/f44e3a674c85aa98cd6fa881e4462b57.pdf), Table 6.2. Supports: Bounded evidence in DIG-K24: Service-unit revenue is distinct from device purchase price.
- [DIG-S15: Adults’ Media Literacy Core Survey 2023 Data Tables](https://www.ofcom.org.uk/siteassets/resources/documents/research-and-data/data/statistics/2024/adults-media-literacy-tracker/adults-media-literacy-core-survey-2023-data-tables?v=330749), M 6A; G 1. Supports: Bounded evidence in DIG-K24: Task perceptions and matched participation outcomes, not determinant coefficients.
- [DIG-S15: Adults’ Media Literacy Core Survey 2023 Data Tables](https://www.ofcom.org.uk/siteassets/resources/documents/research-and-data/data/statistics/2024/adults-media-literacy-tracker/adults-media-literacy-core-survey-2023-data-tables?v=330749), Table 70 M 6A, pp.292–294; unweighted base 3351. Supports: Bounded evidence in DIG-K09: Combined agreement percentage and question.
- [DIG-S16: Adults’ Media Literacy Research 2023 Technical Report](https://www.ofcom.org.uk/siteassets/resources/documents/research-and-data/data/statistics/2024/adults-media-literacy-tracker/adults-media-literacy-tracker-2023-technical-report?v=330751), pp.7–8. Supports: Bounded evidence in DIG-K09: Adult weighting, sample and interview methods.
- [DIG-S15: Adults’ Media Literacy Core Survey 2023 Data Tables](https://www.ofcom.org.uk/siteassets/resources/documents/research-and-data/data/statistics/2024/adults-media-literacy-tracker/adults-media-literacy-core-survey-2023-data-tables?v=330749), Table 74 G 1 pp.307–308; fieldwork header. Supports: Bounded evidence in DIG-K18: Matched device question, levels and 3, 643 base.

**Diagnostic test:**

- **status:** underdetermined
- **mechanism chain:** Bundled device/service attributes and access -> observed preference/use -> insufficient identification of one dominant attribute.
- **strongest rival:** Pocketability alone explains market outcomes, or hardware price alone orders the relevant choices.
- **discriminating observation:** Require dated price distributions and designs separating portability, connectivity, software, performance and ergonomics.
- **evidence sought found:** Parent compared the inspected case claims DIG-K24, DIG-K09, DIG-K18. Source acquisition is documented by their authors; independent sample audits are recorded separately in reviews/.
- **remaining ambiguity:** Unestablished is not false; no current global VR or device-price verdict is claimed.
- **hypothesis timing:** retrospective synthesis

**Counterevidence/rival:** Pocketability alone explains market outcomes, or hardware price alone orders the relevant choices.

**Inference limit:** Unestablished is not false; no current global VR or device-price verdict is claimed.
